                        Remarks: Figures for the year ended 31 March 2022 are shown in brackets
                        註：        截至2022年3月31日止年度之數字於括號內顯示
                        1
                           Sales = Revenue – Licensing Income
                           銷售 = 收入 – 品牌業務收入
                        2
                           Gross Profit = Consolidated Gross Profit – Gross Profit of Licensing Income
                           毛利 = 綜合毛利 – 品牌業務收入毛利
                        3
                           Gold & Platinum refers to gold & platinum products sold by weight basing on international market price, i.e. at non-fixed price
                           黃金及鉑金是根據國際市場價格按重量出售的黃金及鉑金產品（即非定價）
                        4
                           Same store sales growth (“SSSG”) represented a comparison of sales of the same self-operated shop having full day operations in the comparable periods and such data did not include
                           sales of licensed shops and Mainland’s e-commerce business
                           同店銷售增長（         「同店銷售增長」         ）為同一間自營店於可比較期內有完整日營運的銷售額比較，有關數據並不包括品牌店及內地電子商務業務的銷售額

     the Group after the adoption of HKFRS 16. 經調整後未計利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前盈利代表未計利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前盈利不加回使用權資產折舊。管理層認為經調整後未計利
     息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前盈利更能反映本集團採用香港財務報告準則第16號後之營運表現。

1             Luk Fook Holdings (International) Limited 六福集團（國際）有限公司
CORPORATE PROFILE
企業簡介
Established in 1991, Luk Fook Holdings (International) Limited
(the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as
the “Group”) is one of the leading jewellery retailers in Hong Kong
and Mainland. The Group principally engages in the sourcing,
designing, wholesaling, trademark licensing and retailing of a
variety of gold and platinum jewellery and gem-set jewellery
products, with a total of over 3,100 points of sale in 11 countries
and regions across Hong Kong, Macau, Mainland, Malaysia,
Cambodia, the Philippines, Laos, Thailand, the United States,
Canada and Australia.

The Company was listed on the Main Board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (“Stock Exchange”) in May 1997.

六福集團（國際）有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「本
集團」）成立於1991年，是香港及內地主要珠寶零售商之一。本
集團主要從事各類黃鉑金首飾及珠寶首飾產品之採購、設計、
批發、商標授權及零售業務。本集團現時在香港、澳門、內地、
馬來西亞、柬埔寨、菲律賓、老撾、泰國、美國、加拿大及澳洲共
十一個國家和地區擁有逾3,100個零售點。

本公司於1997年5月在香港聯合交易所有限公司（
                       「聯交所」
                           ）主
板上市。


CORPORATE VISION
企業願景
The corporate vision of the Group is to be the “Brand of Hong
Kong, Sparkling the World”. This is also our core brand value. The
Group always puts forth maximum effort for “Lukfook” to reach its
goal of becoming a globally recognised and desired brand, and
as one of the Hong Kong’s most renowned brands in the luxury
industry, the Group commits to crafting the finest jewellery for
customers to further establish its reputation in the international
market.

「香港名牌 • 國際演繹」為本集團的企業願景及品牌核心價值。
本集團一直堅持不懈，盡心竭力使「六福」成為全球認知及喜愛
的品牌。作為香港知名奢侈品牌，本集團致力為全球顧客提供
優質首飾，以進一步確立其於國際市場上的聲譽。

Overview                                                              概覽
During the Year under review, the Group operated a total of 1         於回顧年度內，在多品牌策略下，本集團合共

MARKET ORIENTED STRATEGY
Targeting the mid-to high-end markets, the Group has adopted
comprehensive marketing strategies, and launched diversified
product offerings with exquisite designs, together with meticulous
services to seize the development opportunities in the middle-
class, wedding and generation Z markets.



市場導向策略
針對中高端消費市場，本集團採取全面的市場推廣策略，配合
設計精湛、多元化的產品組合及細緻周到的服務，以抓緊中產、
婚嫁和Z世代市場帶來的發展機遇。



MIDDLE-CLASS MARKET
To cope with our market positioning, we have endeavoured
to further penetrate the middle-class market through various
channels. During the pandemic, the participation rate of running
increased significantly. During the Year under review, the Group
continued to sponsor medals for the Hong Kong and Beijing
marathons to honour marathon finishers, and also organised
promotion activities on the well-known sports mobile application,
“KEEP”, to enhance brand communication, promote sports
culture and raise our brand awareness among the middle-class
population.

The Group is committed to ensuring that its affairs are conducted      本 集 團 致 力 確 保 以 其 企 業 和 管 治 文 化，及 包
in accordance with its corporate and governance culture and            括務實、創新、融合及卓越的價值觀和崇高的

high standards of business ethics, which form the basis of a set of    一套與公司整個業務相關的原則並作為行事
principles that are relevant to the entire business of the Company     的基礎。集團堅信如要達到長遠的業務目標，
and underpin the corporate behaviours. The Group believes that,        必 須 以 誠 信、透 明 和 負 責 任 的 態 度 行 事。在
in the achievement of its long-term objectives, it is imperative to    本 集 團 核 心 價 值 之 引 領 下，董 事 會 肩 負 起 制
act with probity, transparency and accountability. Guided by the       定 本 集 團 宗 旨 及 策 略 方 向 之 領 導 作 用，為 本
Group’s core values, the Board plays a leading role in defining        公 司 定 調 並 塑 造 企 業 文 化，從 而 確 保 集 團 的
the purpose and strategic direction of the Group, setting the tone     的願景、使命、核心價值及策略均與企業文化
and shaping the corporate culture of the Company to ensure the         一致。此外，董事會作為本集團管治架構的核
Group’s vision, mission, core values and strategies remained in        心，致力協助集團強化其根基穩固的願景、使
alignment with its corporate culture. In addition, the Board, as       命及核心價值，及可持續性、問責性的深厚文
the core of our corporate governance structure, is committed           化 乃 至 企 業 管 治 傳 統，並 將 風 險 管 理 及 內 部
to assisting the Group in reinforcing its well-established vision,     監 控 理 念 融 入 企 業 文 化 中。有 關 本 集 團 的 願
mission, core values, as well as a deeply engrained culture of         景、使命、價值觀及管理理念的進一步資料載

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                         企業管治常規
The Company has applied the principles and complied with all           於 回 顧 年 度 內，本 公 司 已 採 用 聯 交 所 證 券 上
code provisions and, where applicable, the recommended best            市規則（ 「上市規則」   ）當時附錄14所載之《企業
practices of the Corporate Governance Code (the “CG Code”) as          管治守則 》  （「企業管治守則」     ）的原則，並已遵
set out in the then Appendix 14 to the Rules Governing the Listing     守所有守則條文及建議最佳常規（如適用）             ，惟
of Securities (the “Listing Rules”) on Stock Exchange during the       下列偏離者除外：
Year under review, except for the following deviation:

Code Provision C.2.1 provides that the roles of chairman               守則條文第C.2.1條規定主席與行政總裁的角
and chief executive should be separate and should not be               色應有區分，並不應由一人同時兼任，使董事
performed by the same individual, so that there is a clear division    會管理與本集團日常業務管理之職責清楚劃
of responsibilities for the management of the Board and the            分，以確保權力和授權之平衡。
day-to-day management of the Group’s business to ensure a
balance of power and authority.

In view of the increasing trend of business deriving from the          由於內地市場帶動業務增長，基於其對「對等
Mainland market, it is believed that Mr. WONG Wai Sheung               階級」之觀念，相信由黃偉常先生擔任本公司

will further enhance the business development of the Group in          務 磋 商 時，進 一 步 提 升 本 集 團 在 內 地 市 場 的

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                  主席及行政總裁
The Chairman leads the Board in the formulation of strategies and     主 席 領 導 董 事 會 制 定 策 略 及 達 成 目 標，並 確
in the achievement of objectives and ensures that all directors       保全體董事對董事會會議上的事項獲適當的
are properly briefed on issues arising at the Board meetings          簡介，並及時獲得足夠、完整及可靠的信息。
and receive adequate, complete and reliable information in a          行政總裁獲授權力，負責本公司業務營運、推
timely manner. The Chief Executive Officer is delegated with the      行本公司之策略，以實踐業務目標。主席及行
authority and responsible for running the Company’s business,         政總裁兩者的職務現時均由執行董事黃偉常
implementing the Company’s strategies in achieving business           先生出任。董事會相信，以現時的管治架構而
objectives. Both the Chairman and the Chief Executive Officer         言（由一人兼任主席及行政總裁，以及大部分
positions are currently held by Mr. WONG Wai Sheung, the              均為非執行董事）足以有效使本公司管理層達
executive director. The Board believes that the current governance    致均衡權責，符合本公司現時的最佳利益。
structure, with one person serving as both the Chairman and
the Chief Executive Officer and the majority being non-executive
Directors, provides an effective balance of power and authority for
the management of the Company and is in the best interest of the
Company at the present stage.

NON-EXECUTIVE DIRECTORS                                               非執行董事
All the non-executive directors are appointed for a term of two       所 有 非 執 行 董 事 之 任 期 為 兩 年，而 其 任 期 可
years, and the appointments are renewable from time to time,          不時重續，惟須於股東週年大會上輪值告退。
subject to retirement by rotation at AGM.

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                  風險管理及內部監控
The Board is responsible for the Company’s systems of risk            董事會負責本公司風險管理及內部監控制
management and internal control and for reviewing their               度，並透過審核委員會每年檢討其成效。
effectiveness annually through the Audit Committee.

The risk management and internal control systems are aligned          風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 制 度 結 合C o m m i t t e e
with the Internal Control Integrated Framework of the Committee       of Sponsoring Organisations of the Treadway
of Sponsoring Organisations of the Treadway Commission                Commission（「 COSO 」）的內部監控綜合框
(“COSO”), which comprises 17 principles under 5 components,           架，該綜合框架由5個部份分17項原則組成，5
including Control Environment, Risk Assessment, Control Activities,   個部份包括控制環境、風險評估、控制活動、
Information and Communication and Monitoring Activities.              訊息與溝通及監控活動。

The risk management and internal control systems are designed         風險管理及內部監控制度旨在管理而非消除
to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve        未 能 達 成 業 務 目 標 的 風 險，而 且 只 能 就 不 會
business objectives, and can only provide reasonable but not          有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對
absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.             的保證。

•     Assessing the residual risks                             •   評估剩餘風險

•     Choosing a risk treatment strategy;                      •  選擇風險應對策略；

•     Establishing a treatment plan; and                       •   訂立應對計劃；及

•     Appointing risk owners                                   •   委任風險負責人

Effectiveness of Monitoring and Reporting                      監察及匯報成效
•      Monitoring and reporting risks periodically; and        •  定期監察及匯報風險；及

•     Setting up an emergent risk reporting mechanism          •   設立突發風險匯報制度

areas for new shops.

and endeavours to promote sustainable development. It                 續 發 展。本 集 團 將 相 關 環 保 策 略 融 入 其 業 務

DIRECTORS’ INTERESTS IN COMPETING                                        董事在競爭業務中之權益
BUSINESS
The Company had 0.71% interest in HKRH as at 31 March 2023.              於2023年3月31日，本公司於香港資源控股中

to fraud or error.

In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors      在擬備綜合財務報表時，董事負責評估 貴集
are responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a     團持續經營的能力，並在適用情況下披露與持
going concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going     續經營有關的事項，以及使用持續經營為會計
concern and using the going concern basis of accounting unless         基礎，除非董事有意將 貴集團清盤或停止經
the directors either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease         營，或別無其他實際的替代方案。
operations, or have no realistic alternative but to do so.

The Audit Committee is responsible for overseeing the Group’s          審核委員會須負責監督         貴集團的財務報告
financial reporting process.                                           過程。

1     GENERAL INFORMATION                                              1   一般資料
      Luk Fook Holdings (International) Limited (the “Company”)            六 福 集 團（國 際）有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）
      was incorporated in Bermuda on 3 September 1996 as                   於1996年9月3日 在 百 慕 達 根 據 百 慕 達
      a company with limited liability under the Companies Act             公 司 法 註 冊 成 立 為 有 限 公 司，其 註 冊
      of Bermuda. The address of its registered office is Victoria         辦事處地址為Victoria Place, 5th Floor
      Place, 5th Floor 31 Victoria Street, Hamilton HM10,                  31 Victoria Street, Hamilton HM10,
      Bermuda.                                                             Bermuda。

      The Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”)             本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主
      are principally engaged in the sourcing, designing,                  要從事各類黃鉑金首飾及珠寶首飾之採
      wholesaling, trademark licensing and retailing of a variety of       購、設計、批發、商標授權及零售業務。
      gold and platinum jewellery, and gem-set jewellery.

      The Company’s shares were listed on the main board of                本公司股份於1997年5月6日在香港聯合
      The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                  交易所有限公司（「聯交所」 ）主板上市。
      Exchange”) on 6 May 1997.

      These consolidated financial statements are presented in             除另有說明者外，此等綜合財務報表均
      Hong Kong dollars (“HK$”), unless otherwise stated, and              以港元（「港元」）呈列，並已於2023年6月
      have been approved for issue by the Board of Directors (the          27日獲董事會（
                                                                                  「董事會」 ）批准刊發。
      “Board”) on 27 June 2023.

3   SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                  3   主要會計政策概要
    POLICIES
    3.1 Principles of consolidation and equity                             3.1 合併原則及權益會計處理
        accounting
        (a)   Business combinations                                           (a)   業務合併
              The acquisition method of accounting is used                          本集團採用購買會計法將所
              to account for all business combinations,                             有 業 務 合 併 入 賬，不 論 是 否
              regardless of whether equity instruments or                           已購買權益工具或其他資
              other assets are acquired. The consideration                          產。購 買 一 間 附 屬 公 司 轉 讓
              transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary                       的代價包括：
              comprises the:

              (i)     fair values of the assets transferred                         (i)     所轉讓資產的公允值

              (ii)    liabilities incurred to the former owners of                  (ii)    被收購業務之前擁有
                      the acquired business                                                 人所產生負債

              (iii)   equity interests issued by the Group                          (iii)   本集團已發行股本權
                                                                                            益

              (iv)    fair value of any asset or liability resulting                (iv)    或有代價安排所產生
                      from a contingent consideration                                       任何資產或負債的公
                      arrangement, and                                                      允值，及

              (v)     fair value of any pre-existing equity interest                (v)     附屬公司任何先前存
                      in the subsidiary.                                                    在的股本權益的公允
                                                                                            值。

              Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and                      在業務合併中所購買的可識
              contingent liabilities assumed in a business                          別資產以及所承擔的負債及
              combination are, with limited exceptions,                             或 然 負 債，初 步 以 其 於 購 買
              measured initially at their fair values at the                        日 期 的 公 允 值 計 量（少 數 例
              acquisition date. The Group recognises any                            外情況除外）。本集團以逐項
              non-controlling interest in the acquired entity                       購 買 基 準，按 公 允 值 或 按 非
              on an acquisition-by-acquisition basis either                         控股權益所佔被收購實體可
              at fair value or at the non-controlling interest’s                    識別資產淨值的比例確認於
              proportionate share of the acquired entity’s net                      被收購實體的任何非控股權
              identifiable assets.                                                  益。

              Acquisition-related costs are expensed as                             購買相關成本於產生時支
              incurred.                                                             銷。

3   SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                3   主要會計政策概要（續）
    POLICIES (Continued)
    3.1 Principles of consolidation and equity                           3.1 合併原則及權益會計處理（續）
        accounting (Continued)
        (b)   Subsidiaries                                                  (b)   附屬公司
              Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                 附屬公司指本集團擁有控制
              entities) over which the Group has control. The                     權 的 所 有 實 體（包 括 結 構 性
              Group controls an entity when the Group is                          實體）。當本集團自參與實體
              exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns                      業務而承擔取得其可變回報
              from its involvement with the entity and has the                    的風險或享有可變回報的權
              ability to affect those returns through its power                   利，並 有 能 力 透 過 其 對 實 體

          greater than its estimated recoverable amount, it is             其可收回金額。
          written down immediately to its recoverable amount.

          Subsequent expenditure is charged to the asset’s                 僅於與項目相關之日後經濟利益
          carrying amount only when it is probably that future             可能流入本集團，且該項目之成本
          economic benefits associated with the item will flow to          能夠可靠計量時，方會自資產賬面
          the Group and the cost of the item can be measured               值扣除其後開支。所有其他維修及
          reliably. All other repair and maintenance costs are             保養成本於產生之財政期間在綜
          expensed in the consolidated income statement                    合損益表支銷。
          during the financial period in which they are incurred.

          If an investment property becomes owner-occupied,                倘投資物業由業主自用，則重新分
          it is reclassified as property, plant and equipment. At          類為物業、廠房及設備。於重新分

          equipment and become its cost and accumulated                    處理成為其成本與累計折舊。倘業
          depreciation for accounting purposes. If an owner-               主自用物業因改變用途而成為投
          occupied property becomes an investment property                 資物業，則重新分類為投資物業。
          because its use has changed, it is reclassified as

        the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An                     進行減值檢測。倘資產之賬面值超

        amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an                 公允值減出售成本與其使用價值
        asset’s fair value less costs of disposal and value                中較高者。就評估減值而言，資產
        in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment,                  乃按最低水平之可獨立識別現金
        assets are grouped at the lowest levels for which                  流入（其大部分獨立於其他資產或
        there are separately identifiable cash inflows which               資產組別（現金產生單位）的現金
        are largely independent of the cash inflows from other             流入）予以分類。出現減值之非金
        assets or groups of assets (cash-generating units).                融資產（商譽除外）將於各報告期
        Non-financial assets other than goodwill that suffered             末檢討是否有撥回減值之可能。
        an impairment are reviewed for possible reversal of the
        impairment at the end of each reporting period.

      3.16 Borrowings                                                       3.16 貸款
          Borrowings are carried initially at fair value, net                  貸款初步按公允值（扣除所產生交
          of transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are                        易成本）確認，其後按攤銷成本列
          subsequently stated at amortised cost; any difference                賬；所得款項（扣除交易成本）與贖
          between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and                  回價值兩者間之任何差額，會以實
          the redemption value is recognised in the consolidated               際利率法於貸款期間內在綜合損
          income statement over the period of the borrowings                   益表中確認。
          using the effective interest method.

          Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are                為建立貸款融資所支付的費用，當
          recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the                   部分或所有融資很可能將被提取
          extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility          時確認為貸款的交易成本。於此情
          will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred                況下，該費用在提取貸款融資前予
          until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is                   以遞延。倘無證據表明部分或所有
          no evidence that it is probable that some or all of the              融資很可能將被提取，則該費用將
          facility will be drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a             作為流動性服務的預付款項資本
          pre-payment for liquidity services and amortised over                化，並在融資相關之期限內攤銷。
          the period of the facility to which it relates.

          Borrowings are removed from the consolidated                         當合約中規定的責任解除、取消或
          balance sheet when the obligation specified in the                   屆滿時，貸款將從綜合資產負債表
          contract is discharged, cancelled or expired. The                    中 剔 除。已 消 除 或 轉 讓 予 另 一 方
          difference between the carrying amount of a financial                的金融負債的賬面值與已支付代價
          liability that has been extinguished or transferred to               （包 括 已 轉 讓 的 任 何 非 現 金 資 產
          another party and the consideration paid, including                  或已承擔的負債）之間的差額，在
          any non-cash assets transferred or liabilities assumed,              綜合損益表中確認。
          is recognised in consolidated income statement.

          Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless              除非本集團有無條件權利可將負
          the Group has an unconditional right to defer                        債結清時間延遲至報告期末後最

          the end of the reporting period.                                     負債。

    (c)   Capital risk management                                         (c)   資本風險管理
          The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                   本集團管理資本的目標為保障本
          safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going                  集團持續運作的能力，從而為股東
          concern in order to provide returns for shareholders                  提供回報、為其他利益相關者提供
          and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain an                利益以及保持理想的資本架構以
          optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.              減低資本成本。

          The Group manages the capital structure and makes                     本集團管理資本架構，並因應經濟
          adjustments to it in the light of changes in economic                 環境的變動作出調整。為保持或調
          conditions. In order to maintain or adjust the capital                整資本架構，本集團可調整支付予
          structure, the Group may adjust the dividend                          股東的股息、發行新股或獲得新的
          payments to shareholders, issue of new shares or                      銀行貸款。
          obtain new bank borrowings.

          The Group also monitors capital on the basis of                       本集團亦以負債資產比率監察其
          gearing ratio. The gearing ratio is calculated as net                 資本。負債資產比率是以淨債項除
          debt divided by total capital. Net debt is calculated as              以總資本計算。淨債項乃按貸款總
          total borrowings (including bank borrowings and gold                  額（包括銀行貸款及黃金借貸）減
          loans) less cash and bank balances. Total capital is                  現金及銀行結餘計算得出。總資本
          calculated as “equity”, as shown in the consolidated                  乃以「權益」
                                                                                     （如綜合資產負債表所
          balance sheet, plus net debt.                                         示）加淨債項計算得出。

          is uncertain. Where the final tax outcome of these                    數額，則有關差額會影響釐定有關

          tax and deferred income tax provisions in the period in               有關之遞延所得稅資產，按管理層
          which such determination is made. Deferred income                     認為未來有可能出現應課稅溢利
          tax assets relating to certain temporary differences                  可用作抵銷該等暫時差異或稅項
          and tax losses are recognised when management                         虧損而確認。
          considers it is probable that future taxable profits will
          be available against which the temporary differences
          or tax losses can be utilised.

      The executive directors and senior management collectively             執行董事及高級管理層獲指定為共同最
      are identified as the chief operating decision-makers                  高營運決策者（「最高營運決策者」）。最
      (“CODM”). The CODM reviews the Group’s internal reporting              高營運決策者通過審閱本集團之內部報

      internal reporting reviewed by the CODM.                               經營分部。

      activities and assesses the performance of the following               務發展及評估下列經營分部之表現：
      operating segments:

      i.       Retailing – Hong Kong, Macau and overseas                     i.     零售－香港、澳門及海外

      CODM assesses the performance of the operating                         最高營運決策者根據分部業績評估經營

      costs, share of results of an associate and corporate income           營分部業績並未包括財務收入及費用、
      and expenses are not included in the results for each                  應佔一家聯營公司業績以及公司收入及
      operating segment that is reviewed by the CODM. Other                  開支。向最高營運決策者提供之其他資
      information provided to the CODM is measured in a manner               料所採用計量方法與綜合財務報表所載
      consistent with that in the consolidated financial statements.         者一致。

30 EMPLOYEE BENEFIT OBLIGATIONS – LONG                               30 僱員福利責任－長期服務金
   SERVICE PAYMENT
      Under the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance, the Group              根據香港《僱傭條例 》，本集團有責任於
      is obligated to make lump sum payments on cessation of           若干情況下，於已服務本集團最少五年
      employment in certain circumstances to certain employees         之若干僱員終止受僱時向其支付一次性
      who have completed at least five years of service with           款項。應付款項乃視乎有關僱員之最後
      the Group. The amount payable is dependent on the                薪金及服務年數而定，並減去本集團退
      employees’ final salary and years of service, and is reduced     休計劃項下及本集團之供款應佔之累算
      by entitlements accrued under the Group’s retirement plan        權益。本集團並無撥出任何資產作為任
      that are attributable to contributions made by the Group.        何餘下責任之資金。於需要支付有關款
      The Group does not set aside any assets to fund any              項時，會自本集團之手頭現金撥款支付
      remaining obligations. The long service payments are paid        長期服務金。
      out from the Group’s cash in hand when such payments are
      required.

      31 March 2022 specifically designated for the Group’s            年：相同）以預計單位信貸法（ 2022年：
      employees was completed by a qualified actuary, Palace           相 同）完 成 專 為 本 集 團 僱 員 而 編 製 之

              Bermuda (as amended), the contributed surplus is distributable to the
              shareholders, provided that the Company would, after the payment of                    司自實繳盈餘派付股息後，須能於負債到期
              dividends out of the contributed surplus, be able to pay its liabilities               時支付債務；或本公司之資產可變現價值據
              as they become due; or the realisable value of the Company’s assets                    此不會少於其債務、其已發行股本及其股份
              would thereby not be less than the aggregate of its liabilities, its                   溢價之總和。就本集團層面而言，實繳盈餘
              issued share capital and its share premium. At the Group level, the                    重新分類為各相關附屬公司之儲備部分。
              contributed surplus is reclassified into its component of reserves of
              the underlying subsidiaries.
